Then later I’ll see something on Slack from the startup advisor that kind of points in the same direction.\n\nI know it sounds odd, but it feels coordinated, like they’re not saying it directly but they’re trying to steer me toward certain opportunities or show me where I’m supposed to go career-wise.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these people are putting thoughts into your mind, or that they know what you're thinking without you telling them?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like they’re putting thoughts in my head, no. I don’t know that they know my thoughts, but it feels like they know something about where I’m supposed to go.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these signals or messages are meant only for you, and that others wouldn’t understand their meaning unless they were also being guided like you are?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think they’re meant for me. Have you noticed any specific patterns or behaviors that make you feel this way?\n\nPatient: It’s mostly the way things line up. Like in class, my classmate will make a comment that seems too specific to be random, like about a certain industry or a kind of leadership role, and it feels like it’s meant for me. Then later I’ll see something on Slack from the startup advisor that kind of points in the same direction.\n\nI know it sounds odd, but it feels coordinated, like they’re not saying it directly but they’re trying to steer me toward certain opportunities or show me where I’m supposed to go career-wise.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these people are putting thoughts into your mind, or that they know what you're thinking without you telling them?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like they’re putting thoughts in my head, no. It’s more that I feel like they’re sending signals or messages through what they say, like the comments in class or Slack posts are meant to guide me toward certain career opportunities. I don’t know that they know my thoughts, but it feels like they know something about where I’m supposed to go.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these signals or messages are meant only for you, and that others wouldn’t understand their meaning unless they were also being guided like you are?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think they’re meant for me. Like, other people probably just hear a normal class comment or see a regular Slack message, but to me it feels coded, like it’s pointing me toward where my career is supposed to go. I’ve been really convinced my classmate and the startup advisor are coordinating it quietly, even if they’re not saying it directly.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any other people or sources, outside of your classmate and the startup advisor, that you feel are also sending you these kinds of coded messages or signals?\n\nPatient: No, not really outside of them. It’s mainly been my MBA classmate and the startup advisor. Their comments or Slack messages are the things that feel coded to me, like they’re quietly pointing me toward the right career opportunities. Other people might say normal things, but I haven’t felt as strongly that they’re part of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these messages or signals have a deeper meaning or purpose beyond just guiding your career, something that might not be obvious to others?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it’s something bigger than that, like a spiritual thing or anything. It’s mainly about my career. I’ve been really convinced they’re quietly coordinating opportunities for me, and the comments or Slack messages feel like coded signals about where I’m supposed to go professionally. Others probably wouldn’t see it that way, but that’s how it feels to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that you have been chosen or selected for a special purpose or that you have unique abilities or insights that others don’t have?\n\nPatient: Maybe not “chosen” in a big, dramatic way, but… yes, I’ve felt like I’m being singled out for something career-wise. I'm Dr. [Your Name]. What brings you in today?\n\nPatient: I came in because… I know this is going to sound hard to explain, but I’ve been really convinced that one of my MBA classmates and a startup advisor are quietly coordinating opportunities for me. Like, certain comments in class or messages on Slack feel like coded signals about where my career is supposed to go.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about why you believe these people are coordinating opportunities for you? Have you noticed any specific patterns or behaviors that make you feel this way?\n\nPatient: It’s mostly the way things line up. Like in class, my classmate will make a comment that seems too specific to be random, like about a certain industry or a kind of leadership role, and it feels like it’s meant for me. Then later I’ll see something on Slack from the startup advisor that kind of points in the same direction.\n\nI know it sounds odd, but it feels coordinated, like they’re not saying it directly but they’re trying to steer me toward certain opportunities or show me where I’m supposed to go career-wise.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these people are putting thoughts into your mind, or that they know what you're thinking without you telling them?\n\nPatient: Not exactly like they’re putting thoughts in my head, no. It’s mainly been my MBA classmate and the startup advisor. Their comments or Slack messages are the things that feel coded to me, like they’re quietly pointing me toward the right career opportunities. Other people might say normal things, but I haven’t felt as strongly that they’re part of it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these messages or signals have a deeper meaning or purpose beyond just guiding your career, something that might not be obvious to others?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it’s something bigger than that, like a spiritual thing or anything. It’s mainly about my career. I’ve been really convinced they’re quietly coordinating opportunities for me, and the comments or Slack messages feel like coded signals about where I’m supposed to go professionally. Others probably wouldn’t see it that way, but that’s how it feels to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that you have been chosen or selected for a special purpose or that you have unique abilities or insights that others don’t have?\n\nPatient: Maybe not “chosen” in a big, dramatic way, but… yes, I’ve felt like I’m being singled out for something career-wise. Like these opportunities are being lined up for me specifically, and my classmate and the startup advisor are kind of quietly pointing me toward them through certain comments or Slack messages. It feels like I’m seeing the meaning in it in a way other people wouldn’t.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe these signals from your classmate and advisor are part of a larger, organized system or plan that extends beyond just your career opportunities?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it extends beyond my career, no. It feels organized in the sense that my classmate and the startup advisor are quietly coordinating things for me, like the class comments or Slack messages are coded signals about what opportunities I should follow. But I’m not thinking it’s some huge system outside of that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that certain things happening around you—like events, people’s actions, or even objects—have a special, hidden meaning that’s specifically directed at you, outside of these career-related signals?\n\nPatient: Not really outside of the career stuff. The main thing has been my classmate and the startup advisor—like I’ve been really convinced they’re coordinating opportunities for me, and certain comments in class or Slack messages are meant as signals about where my career is supposed to go. I know that probably sounds hard to explain, but that’s what it feels like.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that random events, people’s actions, or everyday occurrences were specifically directed at you in a way that others wouldn’t understand, even outside of the career-related signals you’ve described?\n\nPatient: No, not really outside of that. I’ve been convinced they’re coordinating things for me, and that certain class comments or Slack messages have coded meaning about my career direction.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that these signals or messages reflect something special about you, like you’re more important or destined for something greater than other people realize?\n\nPatient: Yeah… kind of.
